**[pbu20-0280]** — Light duties worsen schedule; leave rules obstruct hoped-for preparation time. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Work has been an absolute hell. I’ve been put on light duty and they’ve accommodated me as needed but sadly the accommodations just made things worse and absolutely destroyed my schedule in the process. Since being thrown into an entirely new and essentially more demanding schedule, it made a HUGE impact on when I’m able to get help on certain things and get things done. Making it feel nearly impossible since I need at least 12 hours of sleep on my days off... I tried fighting it and it just doesn’t work. :/  Work also hasn’t been the nicest since I’ve gone on light duty. Which I just don’t even want to get into that.  
> 
> With all of that, I would love nothing more than to be able to take my leave at least 2 weeks early so I can feel ready for baby’s arrival but unfortunately the kind of maternity leave I’m offered is ONLY allowed to be taken the day baby is born. Which is unfortunate. The only way to go out early is if it is medically needed. Which I would have to run through my OB and I’m just not sure if she would do it or not. I’d only be asking for two weeks and a major part of it is due to the excruciating back and pelvic pain. Which that was the reason I got put on light duty to begin with. :( just not sure what to do about that….

**[pbu20-0282]** — Potential single mother wants a savings target and plans more work during pregnancy. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> I am trying to prepare. I am 6 weeks and my baby seems healthy with a strong heartbeat, I know miscarriage is still possible but my chances have dropped statistically. If I go through with my pregnancy I’ll be a single mum. How much should I save before baby comes? I am preparing for working a lot starting now while pregnant. I don’t want to make this harder for baby and I and if I have financials sorted it will definitely be less stressful. Thank you

**[pbu20-0283]** — Medication and remedies fail; repeated hospital-fluid costs shape consideration of THC. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I am almost at the end of my first trimester and have been experiencing severe hyper emesis gravidarum and nothing works. I’ve tried Zofran, every over-the-counter remedy, ginger and nothing works. I have lost so much weight and I can’t keep any fluids down and I also can’t afford to go to the hospital every single day to get fluids. I’m debating on trying THC to help with nausea because I am so scared of losing this baby and I have tried and everything under the sun and my doctors don’t know how to help me anymore.

**[pbu20-0285]** — After reported miscarriage misdiagnosis, home Doppler fails to reassure between appointments. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I had a scare at the beginning where they misdiagnosed a miscarriage. This is my first pregnancy and it really left a scar I can't seem to shake. I bought an at home doppler, and can't seem to locate the heartbeat. I know I know it's not recommended exactly for this reason, which is why I've only used it twice. My next appointment is on August 18th and that feels so long away.

**[pbu20-0303]** — Author reports missing enhanced-leave eligibility while carrying mortgage and household bills. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> The timing is off, my job only offers enhanced maternity after 3 years of employment… by the time it comes to my due date I will have only been there 2 years and 8 months.   
> I have a mortgage with my partner. Our beautiful first home! We pay £1100 in mortgage, and all the rest of the bills and insurances add to £2000 in total. Leave us both with around £1300 in personal spends.   
> But… he smokes a lot of weed- it’s his thing. But it’s a huge drain on our resources.   
> Regardless of the weed when I got on to ssmp it’s like £167 a week!

**[pbu20-0304]** — Finances, work scheduling and partner's cannabis use raise fears about newborn care. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m scared I will loose my home.   
> I’m scared my partner won’t make changes.   
> I’m scared he will get us in debt with his parents. I’m also scared if I can even go back to my job of 45 hours a week with us both working in the same place how will we create a rota that our managers agree to. I’m scared to leave him alone with the baby if he doesn’t give up the weed. Have I made a grave mistake?

**[pbu20-0309]** — Financially driven termination conflicts with attachment and demands of immediate return to class. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Seeing the ultrasound and how developed the baby is has left me completely depressed. My boyfriend and I are not in the financial position to raise a child at all and I am very set on getting this abortion, but I feel so so so guilty and ashamed. I really wish i didn't have to have an abortion and I so desperately want to continue with the pregnancy. I feel awful and it's all I can think about. How do you get over the guilt of an abortion? 
> 
> What makes this harder for me is that I just started my college semester and I have to resume to class immediately and keep up with all the assignments of a full time student.

**[pbu20-0314]** — No ultrasound available; author declines uncovered blood tests before scheduled care. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I went to a clinic and they said there wasn’t anything they could do because they didn’t have an ultrasound tech at the moment. The lady said that I am most likely having a miscarriage. She just kept saying I’m so sorry and that was it. Then she said they could do a blood test and then another in a few days. I said no because I’m supposed to have an appointment on the 10th and that place wasn’t covered by my insurance.

**[pbu20-0335]** — Hostility and exclusion from family food influence decision about fourth pregnancy. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> My husband has been awful to me lately. Tonight I’m feeling it’s the last straw. He bought my kids and him food but not me. I asked my son to get me a chicken nugget and he told him no look at her (I’m fat) she can get up and cook. This is typical stuff but I’m like I can’t have my 4th kid with this man. I told him if I don’t have it it’s 1000% on him and the horrible way he’s treating me. I told him I’d never forgive him and we’re over after this. I can’t even tell if her cares…

**[pbu20-0340]** — Registry changes and anticipated conversations make twin loss part of ongoing preparation. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I keep remembering every ultrasound and seeing him and wondering what they were both gonna look like, if he was gonna look more like me or his dad, when I sleep I dream of them both still, I think of all the twin things I have in my registry and how I have to change that, the people I’ll run into, the people who call me twins because I was having twins and the thought of having to hold it together when I tell them one of them is dead.

**[pbu20-0341]** — Public announcement and relationship conflict complicate pregnancy and custody decisions. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> i don’t want to have an abortion because people already know about our pregnancy but i don’t think i can do this unfortunately. it feels like every other day we argue about nonsense and im left feeling alone and tired. i’m considering just giving him full custody because he makes enough money to support a child and himself and he very clearly doesn’t care about me. i don’t want to have a family with him and i just want to move on and be happy. i’ve told him how unhappy i am multiple times and instead of fixing our problems(i know now they’re not fixable) he just tells me “don’t be with me then” which we barely are even together. he is convinced unfortunately that because im pregnant we HAVE to be together and im over it. im just so tired of feeling worthless and depressed, my baby doesn’t deserve it and i just want to erase him from my life.

**[pbu20-0344]** — Grief during pregnancy compounds disappointment with an unreliable friend. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I lost my Mum 5 months ago im now 21 weeka pregnant the friend that was around for the funeral that I paid for the ticket for her to attend has no dissapeared for a month she was saying she was going to come everyday day at one point but the day would come and it would be an excuse then she said it would either be that weekend or the weekend after both weekends came she didnt even bother message to say she wasnt coming I then post my son up she then sends a whats app saying how am I and how is the pregancy I then ignore it because like really what happened the whole of that time she the. Messages couple days later saying she doesn't know why I opened ignored her how I must really hate her how we both dealing with things that's might be different but she's dealing with things which are a married man and not had work In years how can she compare losing my mum to her problems am I over reacting

**[pbu20-0356]** — Young partner asks for outside perspective on finances and education after pregnancy discovery. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `partner`).

> We are torn between keeping the baby and having to get an abortion. Me 19m am just getting situated in my career and just started making decent money. We are just worried about the financial situation and how we can both survive in the current state of the world. She also has plans to go school and be a hygienist. My girlfriend just wants some outside advice from some adults. Any advice is helpful thank you very much.

**[pbu37-0024]** — Author wants medical documentation to stop work before induction. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `experienced_mother`).

> My care team already ensured they can induce me at 39 weeks, but I also want to have a note to put me out of work 2 weeks before this. My job does not have a formal paperwork to fill out for this, but they just want a note saying the dr approves/recommends that I’m out of works that 2 weeks.

**[pbu37-0030]** — Pregnant sixteen-year-old asks for an overview of pregnancy and raising a baby. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I need recommendations and tips and my parents said they will help me with money which is good so like im not asking for money but I need like just advice as to what the whole pregnancy stage will be like bc ik im going to drop out of 11th grade and my boyfriend has good amount of money so I might live with him but I just need general advice as to raising a baby, dont judge please just keep this about the baby, tyyyy :)

**[pbu37-0037]** — Author buys a UTI test after burning on urination. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I drank a bunch of water but I was still concerned so I ran to the store and got a UTI test. I just took the test and it did not burn at all when I peed, but the leukocyte portion showed positive, and nitrites negative.

**[pbu37-0044]** — Insurance and notice requirements keep a burned-out healthcare worker in twelve-hour shifts. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I would leave sooner but my husband is finishing graduate school and I need the insurance. I also have to give 3 months notice to my job so that is approximately how much time I have left till my maternity leave starts. I plan on going back to work for 3 months after my maternity leave then quitting!

**[pbu37-0045]** — Work-related intrusive thought and a month off that failed to reset exhaustion. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’ve also had the thought of getting into a fender bender while driving to work which I would never actually do so I completely understand what you mean. I actually took a month of scheduled paid time off in July and while it was amazing, I was surprised that I didn’t feel nearly as reset when I returned as I have in the past. I think that really made me realize how burned out I actually am.

**[pbu37-0080]** — Different symptom patterns and a previous loss prompt fear of missed miscarriage before a scan. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Hi, I'm currently 11+4, been experiencing nausea, fatigue etc since week 6, this is all new to me as my first child, I didn't get any symptoms at all, I had a miscarriage back in November so my anxiety it quite bad atm, we had a private scan at 8 weeks and everything looked good, I'm nearing my 12 week scan and I'm scared they're not gonna find a heartbeat and it's a missed miscarriage, for those who have experienced a MM (im sorry that you have) did you still have symptoms or did they disappear?

**[pbu37-0087]** — Limited maternity pay makes two mortgages and basic household costs feel unmanageable. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> 6. Mat leave… only paid 90% for the first 6 weeks and much, much less for the remaining 37. We wouldn‘t even be able to pay for our mortgages let alone heating and electric or food. everything would be so much harder.

**[pbu37-0088]** — Wanted unexpected pregnancy raises financial, relationship and future-fertility fears. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> We don’t know what to do, we want the baby feel excited and lucky but terrified. We are so early into our own relationship. Is it fair to have a baby in our situation? Is it selfish? We have no money for holidays, school trips, clothes, buggy’s and everhjbg a child needs. It would probably be bullied for not having anything new or decent. It would never see the world like me, it wouldn’t have a good or healthy diet. I just feel like crying as it’s hit me that I can’t give a child a good life, but then what difference would 5 years even make to that? not like salaries are going up. Anyone in a situation like this and made it work? Would an abortion now affect our fertility future?

**[pbu37-0105]** — Pregnancy and surgical recovery collide with toddler care despite partner leave and practical help. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `experienced_mother`).

> With that said, I'm so tired. I feel like I was so tired before all of this and now, I'm just physically and mentally exhausted. My husband took leave from work the first week and some change after my surgery and he works from home so he's able to pick him up for naps/meals since I cannot lift him.  With that said, I've had no time to just be. My poor son was so upset the first few days after being home from my surgery so I tried to be around and playing from the get go.
> 
> I'm in pain, on top of terrible back pain from pregnancy, and I'm trying to keep it together. I hardly sleep at night. Things still need to be done and one thing I realized is that...the world doesn't stop. It just keeps going. 
> 
> I should be thankful and so happy about this diagnosis. And I AM. But it just feels like, okay well that's that! Back to normal now!

**[pbu37-0108]** — Mother reports a partner using three months of parental leave for games and exercise rather than childcare. (stage: `looking_back`; speaker: `new_mother`).

> After I had the baby he took 3 months parental leave with me and he did zero child care. He never helped with night wakings. He stayed up till 3 am playing videos games, wake up at 11am go straight to working out for a hour or so go for a run then come back take a 45 min shower then either by the time he was done with his “plans” it was already around 3 pm every day. I would ask for help but never got it.

**[pbu37-0116]** — Partner seeks guidance or a book for fear of losing identity and hating fatherhood. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `partner`).

> I’ve always been an anxious person and this has just ramped everything up to 11. I know everyone says things change because it’s your kid, but I am so afraid of the unknown/change that I’m terrified that I’m going to hate being a father. I’ll be the best father I can possible be, but I’m so scared that I have just ruined the rest of my life.
> 
> I’m hoping someone has gone through similar feelings and can guide me in the right direction, or can point me towards a book that might be able to help me get past this fear.

**[pbu37-0122]** — NICU transfer requires urgent travel and weeks of temporary accommodation. (stage: `looking_back`; speaker: `partner`).

> Then an ultrasound of her head revealed a brain bleed, then another ultrasound a bit later uncovered that the brain bleed was ongoing (4-4), and that our girl was being transferred via helicopter to a level 4 NICU 2 hours away. We packed the car and followed immediately.  Spent the night in the NICU room without clean clothes to change into the next day. 
> 
> It’s now been a month since her transfer, we spent a couple weeks at a hotel, a couple weeks at an Airbnb, and now we are staying at the Ronald McDonald House.

**[pbu37-0124]** — Partner's denied leave forces solo international travel to discuss a new pregnancy with a child placed for adoption. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> My eldests dad is also still my current partner. His Boss after promising he'd have the time off is now refusing to give him said time off. So not only will I be flying a total of 13 hours into 2 foreign countries solo, around 15 weeks pregnant with a medical lift limit of 20 pounds but I will also now have to keep my hormones in check entirely alone through this conversation with our eldest and idk what to do, how to prep, what to expect or how to not get overwhelmed as every time I even think about her being upset by this news I am gutted like slightly sobbing in the back at work.

**[pbu37-0147]** — Author wants a low-effort pregnancy journal that can hold saved test strips. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> FTM, thinking about starting a pregnancy journal or buying a little keepsake book to help hold me accountable. I still have all my HCG test stips that I feel I should tape somewhere. Nothing too intense otherwise I won’t do it LOL. Anyone have recommendations??

**[pbu37-0164]** — Author lists housing, purchases and practical preparation completed despite inconsistent paternal support. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Anyway, despite all this negativity and drama which has drained me (alongside pregnancy hormones!) I've managed to:
> 
> 	•	Buy a house, as a solo ftb;  
> 	•	Buy all new furniture for that house;   
> 	•	Decorate his nursery;  
> 	•	Buy all of his babies clothes;  
> 	•	Research and buy his pram / car seat;  
> 	•	Pack our hospital bags;  
> 	•	Sort out my maternity leave;

> 
> I'm so proud of myself for managing to stay positive in a situation that I would have thought would break me. Though my family and friends have been amazing and so supportive throughout.

**[pbu37-0182]** — Moving home for birth support brings unwanted delivery-room and month-long visitor assumptions. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I am moving back to my home country for my birth because of financial reasons and extended family support. One of the difficult situations I was already navigating was dealing with her and her lack of boundaries with me. As I came to see her, she started already breaking my boundaries, even to the extend of implying she's going to be in the delivery room, moving back to the United States with me, and staying with me the first month of my child's life... While touching my belly, and of course criticizing my looks and comparing her pregnancy to mine, and how what I'm planning sounds stupid.

**[pbu37-0183]** — Prebooked international travel triggers panic despite obstetric reassurance. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> But I am so incredibly anxious. I have a panic disorder and I just got off my lexapro in December after 5years and my mind is racing. We leave on Tuesday and it just set it right now. What if I can’t do it? We spent so much money. My husband is so excited and I keep telling him I am too but I want to die on the inside. I don’t even know why I’m so anxious. I guess I’m just looking for support or similar stories.

**[pbu37-0184]** — Late-pregnancy surname decisions feel coerced by an inconsistent ex-partner seeking involvement. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> The closer I get to her due date, the more I’m freaking out and realizing that I’ve been comprising on what I want and feel is the right decision for the sake of not wanting to upset him and hopes one day we would get back together. I don’t feel comfortable giving her his last name. I’m scared to give her his last name. But now I’m even more scared because I buried myself with telling him yes again, and now I want to back out of it and have it fully be only my last name. He hasn’t been there for me. He left. I have spent almost every day crying and apologizing to my baby, I have been severely depressed. The only thing he has done for me during this pregnancy is buy me fast food and some groceries, he has shown up for me only a handful of times when I told him I needed him because I was having a breakdown, the only thing he’s done in preparation for her is build her crib and take the car seat out of the box. He literally is planning on being high at her birth. He won’t get sober for his daughter. I don’t trust his family because I have seen and heard awful things and stories. I do believe him when he says he will be a father to her. But I don’t feel like he deserves to have the last name but I feel wrong in telling him that I want it to be my last name.

**[pbu37-0187]** — Employer offers light duty, but obstetric office reportedly declines documentation because nothing is wrong. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I said that my manager/director was absolutely willing to accommodate light duty to keep me in my actual role and that I would need paperwork filled out from my OB. She said that since there’s nothing actually wrong they aren’t able to fill out any paperwork.

**[pbu37-0192]** — A prior agreement about financial readiness becomes emotionally difficult after a positive test. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> We’ve always said if we couldn’t support it financially that it would be a big reason for us not to keep it. That’s always been a big one for me. He’s still on that track. Which I understand, he says our finances aren’t well (which he’s right), that he’s not ready for him to have to be put second, and that he’s not ready for it to not just be us to anymore. Everything is completely understandable. But I’m honestly torn. I’ve always told myself I’d never keep a baby for selfish reasons, just because I know I want kids someday. I’ve always wanted to make sure I can support a child emotionally, mentally, financially, and physically. But my heart can’t stop breaking at the thought of abortion.

**[pbu37-0198]** — Referral and insurance barriers block dating care in a pregnancy estimated by family at six months. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I have been trying to set up an obgyn appointment but with my insurance it is seemingly impossible. Either places will say they accept my insurance and then tell me they don’t once I call. Or that I need a referral from my pcp. The problem is I haven’t had insurance and years and think I met my pcp only once around 7 years ago. I am trying to get my insurance now to go through Kaiser as I have experience with them and their facilities. My main question is; if I go to an urgent care and explain that I am pregnant and worried due to past miscarriages would I be able to get an ultrasound at an urgent care or ER without needing some referral?

**[pbu37-0200]** — Vanishing twin prevents usable NIPT results and removes expected early reassurance. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> We sent the blood work off and everything. It came back Inconclusive. They didn't even run the test at all because they couldn't tell the twins fetal DNA apart. So now we are simply waiting until the anatomy scan to know any chromosomal makeup of our remaining baby. Which will be in another month or so. We do know it's a boy because of a private ultrasound we had done. But other than that we are in the dark entirely on risks for this baby and it is so terrifying that the possibility of knowing early got ripped away from us. I guess I'm looking for support, advice, etc to deal with this anxiety. Thank you ❤️

**[pbu37-0201]** — Author feels pressured to terminate a wanted third pregnancy while grieving a close friend's death. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> The problem is he has said that he would be happy to have a third a couple of years down the line once we have moved and are more financially stable etc but I’m 36 this year and I don’t want to have a baby when I’m 38/39 years old. For me, it’s really now or never and I of course don’t want to ruin our marriage by keeping a baby that he ultimately isn’t happy about us having. However I also don’t want to feel like I’m being pressured into making a decision I don’t want to make because of concerns about money that actually may not be so bad. I don’t think that there is ever a perfect time to have a baby and actually both our son and daughter were born at times when we weren’t exactly financially prepared.. but we made it work. 
> 
> I can’t have this conversation with him as he completely shuts me down and says that it just doesn’t work and we won’t be able to afford it etc and I just feel like he’s basically not even trying to work out a way in which it could happen as he has made up his mind that it will be too hard.

**[pbu37-0223]** — Unexpected pregnancy arrives just after kinship foster placement, expanding a household already caring for five children. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `experienced_mother`).

> Ok, my to my point, sorry for the side ramble. I think the universe is playing tricks on me, so I have an appointment on Monday, and based on my last period, I am 9 weeks pregnant. Also, I have not been on BC since I had a miscarriage in 2019 because I started having anovulatory cycles, so I didn't think I would get pregnant and figured I would start perimenopause soon. Has anyone else had a baby at the worst time, and it work out? We are financially comfortable, so it is not a financial concern it's just 6 kids is a lot of kids, but so is 5.

**[pbu37-0229]** — Daycare waitlists and a larger mortgage constrain planned leave and amplify separation fears. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> I’m anxious because we didn’t sign up baby for daycare the moment we found out we are pregnant. SIL #2 has had her baby on a waitlist nearing 2 years now. SIL #1 can afford nanny. We just bought an expensive house in the city, so I can’t take off a year like I would’ve liked to because the mortgage is nearly double what our first home’s was. Im anxious about finances after having just paid off my student loans. I’m anxious to leave a 4 month old with anyone and go back to work after baby comes in October.

**[pbu37-0241]** — Conflicting results and diagnostic uncertainty make an emergency bill feel especially frustrating. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Glad I'm gonna see that bill afer a shitty 4 hour wait to tell me I'm either very early, (3 weeks which would make sense) or MMC or Ectopic...! 
> 
> I'm just so frustrated and confused! I'm still brown spotting today, and through the weekend with some minor cramps...if it is MMC I just want it over with!

**[pbu37-0256]** — An emergency at work exposes pregnancy to family without the author's chosen disclosure. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> My boss called my sister and told her I was pregnant and hemorrhaging. She called my parents and told them. 
> 
> I had not told my family yet that I was pregnant. I am 30 years old with a finance who is my emergency contact and who was already aware of what was going on and meeting me at the ER. 
> 
> I feel my privacy had been extremely violated. I am not very close with my sister and again, I have an emergency contact and she is not it. So not only did he call someone who my work is NOT authorized to call but he told my entire family I was pregnant before I was ready to do so. 
> 
> I cannot get over it. I feel so hurt and I just dont know how to move forward from this. I keep seeing videos of people telling their families and how happy they are and I feel such envy that my opportunity was stolen from me by someone I'm not even close with. I dont know how to get over these feelings. 
> 
> Does anyone have any advice?

**[pbu37-0262]** — Spotting prompts an immediate flight purchase and uncertainty about emergency care versus calling the next morning. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m currently on a flight home, im so scared that I literally bought a plane ticket not even an hour after I saw the blood. I’m wondering if I should go to the emergency as soon as I land or wait till tomorrow Monday morning and call my obgyn. I don’t think I’ve had much bleeding right now, I haven’t felt anything, but I’m also very scared to go check. :(
> 
> What do yall think would be best, ER tonight or calling my OB tomorrow morning?

**[pbu37-0263]** — Author distinguishes emotional readiness and lifestyle timing from financial security, relationship stability and family help. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> The biggest reason we’re considering terminating isn’t financial or relationship-related. We both have stable jobs, live together in NYC, and know we want to spend our lives together. We also haven’t told either of our families yet, but we’re certain they would want us to keep the pregnancy and would be incredibly supportive. We know we’d have a strong village to help us if we became parents.
> 
> The difficult part is that neither of us feels ready to leave the stage of life we’re in. Right now, our lives revolve around friends, hobbies, and travel. We see friends several times a week, travel 5–6 times a year, and none of our close friends are planning to have kids anytime soon. We know becoming parents would change our priorities and lifestyle much sooner than we expected. At the same time, we’re confident that if we chose to keep the pregnancy, we would step up and be loving, responsible parents.

**[pbu37-0269]** — Partner attends appointments, saves for longer leave and participates in preparation despite lacking excitement. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> He’s shown up to every Dr appointment, often times even asking more questions than I do during the appointments. He put a decent amount of money aside to ensure I could take a longer maternity leave if I wanted to care for baby boy. He actively participated in our baby shower (jack and Jill style), even paid for a photographer for us (that I found) and posted all of the photos to his Facebook page after we received all of them

**[pbu37-0272]** — Time and cost of repeated blood tests make early symptom assessment difficult. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I called my obgyn and since I’m not yet at the 8 week mark, if they were to get me in I would have to go in every other day to get blood drawn for tests, and I don’t have the time nor the money for that. I could also go to the ER if the pain were to get worse, but at this stage, they wouldn’t even be able to see much. I don’t have women around me that I can ask for advice, so I’m hoping someone here is willing to share their experience and maybe help me realize whether this is normal or I should seek immediate help.

**[pbu37-0310]** — Fear of pregnancy, costs and a religious mother's reaction delays taking a test. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Im the (18 y/o) daughter of a very religious mother. Love her to death but I think she would kill me if I was pregnant. The reason I think I may be is because im quite late to my period, but theres also a chance thats because I recently stopped taking my birth control because it was causing issues. I havent taken a test because im far to anxious so im kinda just waiting it out. Im not financially stable enough to even give birth let alone keep the child so id prefer to get an abortion if I am in fact pregnant, but as previously stated, my mom is super religious… please help me figure out what to do because im so scared.q

**[pbu37-0316]** — Insurance denial then a sample/fax mismatch delay genetic testing even after author agrees to self-pay. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Fast forward two weeks, insurance denied it because it isn’t medically necessary. At that point I said fine, I will pay out of pocket if it means we can start the testing. GC said testing would start that Monday. 8 days later, I’ve heard nothing so I reach out. Unfortunately they didn’t connect the fax to start testing to my sample so it never was started but they assured me it would be ready in 3-5 days. We’re now past that window, and tomorrow makes 4 weeks of waiting.

**[pbu37-0317]** — Unanswered referral and insurance resources coincide with little baby-shower response and a sense of having no village. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I was supposed to get a referral to behavioral health but no one called, none of the resources my insurance offered responded, I have no village, all my family lives too far away and so do my friends, the friends I have close by don’t care much anymore and only 4 people responded to my baby shower invite so I’m likely gonna cancel it.  I dont truly know why I created a registry when no one is going to really be supporting us anyways.

**[pbu37-0319]** — Financial and leave uncertainty complicate considering separation during a wanted pregnancy. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> This baby was very much wanted and planned. I think that this is the icing on the cake for me. I love my husband but the way he treats me has me at the worst emotional low I have ever experienced. I miss being in my own home, I miss cleanliness, I miss emotional security, and I miss my family. The idea of moving is daunting. I don't know how I will afford the initial move and I don't know what I will do when I take maternity leave. Has anyone seperated from their partner when pregnant and come out happier on the other end?

**[pbu37-0320]** — A maternity-benefit waiting period makes pregnancy timing a choice about interim costs and unplanned emergencies. (stage: `trying`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> My insurance confirmed with me in writing that even if I conceive prior to April the 2nd, my benefits will still become active on that date. So I know that insurance will cover the delivery, but I’ll have to pay out of pocket if I become pregnant prior to April 2nd for OB visits/care. I know I can probably manage to pay for a few month out of pocket for care but what if something emergent happens because of pregnancy? Like a hospital visit or a miscarriage. Or if theres something wrong with the baby I’ll need more testing for. I want to trust my body that everything will be okay and I can get pregnant and just wait it out until April the 2nd but I also feel irresponsible and part of me thinks I should wait. I really want a baby now and want to start trying. Thoughts?

**[pbu37-0327]** — Comparison with another pregnant household member intensifies shame and unwanted thoughts about abortion. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> After my brother singled me out, I just went to my room and I broke down. I’m trying I swear I am, but I guess he doesn’t see that. I’ve been going to school as well and trying to continue my education through this pregnancy and have income that is helping out with bills and other expenses for the house. So it’s not like I’m completely useless. But he’s making me feel like I am. I just felt so embarrassed and not seen. He made me feel like my pregnancy and baby were an inconvenience. My thoughts kind of spiralled and I even went as far as to thinking about abortion. I’m even slowly starting to feel unwanted resentment towards his gf as I feel like she’s setting expectations that I feel like I just can’t meet right now even though I know it’s not her fault at all.

**[pbu37-0329]** — Author buys maternity shirts to make a pregnancy after loss feel celebratory. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Even against my better judgement I purchased some maternity shirts that I’m really hoping get use! It was a great deal I found; hoping this helps me feel celebratory.

**[pbu37-0338]** — Author imagines paid maternity benefits relieving financial strain despite extensive preparation and partner support. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I can’t help but daydream about how much easier I could breathe if I was in a country that offered paid maternity benefits.  Maybe it wouldn’t feel like there’s bricks on my chest everyday. Maybe I wouldn’t sit here and wrack my brain everyday about what I could do differently or better.

**[pbu37-0340]** — Major job offer, physical travel, benefit uncertainty and disclosure timing complicate whether to continue pregnancy. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> I work in outside sales and that requires me to visit offices with samples and present them. It also requires extra social functions such as dinners, happy hours, etc. What really throws a wrench into things is that I am in the midst of finalizing a job offer which would take my base salary from 125k to 180k... life changing, nevermind the commission. I have already received a verbal offer and we discussed a start date of 1/2. The job is technically remote but it requires visits to clients multiple times a week within the NYC area. Sometimes pulling heavy samples across the city, othertimes leading social functions after hours. I would also have to do 2 weeks of training at the main office in another state at the start. I am terrified of losing the opportunity or not performing as expected due to the pregnancy. I wish I was a few months into the job.. but I guess we don't always get to choose when these things happen. I haven't received the benefits package yet at the new company but I am extermely focused on understanding the lapse in insurance, and the best way to cover it (cobra, market place ins, etc). I am also aware that to qualify for FMLA I need to work for an employer for a year which also worries me as I have not reviewed the new maternity policy yet for the new company. Staying at my current role is not an option because the job opportunity is too financially beneficial. Beyond that I am struggling with when is the right time to notify the new company. I would be 16/17 weeks during the training and likley showing or just starting to show. I should be receiving the written offer the week before the scheduled abortion. I am currently 190 lbs and have considered if I can maintain a healthy first trimester and wear oversized clothes, I can maybe count on hiding it and notifiying them later. I don't want to be deceitful and I know legalllyyyyy they can't rescind the offer based on pregancy. The woman I'd be reporting to has kids and that is a plus but I am so scared I'm going to ruin things or upset them.

**[pbu37-0341]** — Career opportunity and desired motherhood arrive together; author prepares physically while keeping an abortion appointment as a decision point. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> I am feeling resentful and sad that I am in this position because I want to be a mom but I am extremely ambitious and don't want to risk this opportunity and the timing couldn't be worse. Beyond this, this income is exactly what I wanted in order to feel comfortable having a baby in NYC without family help.. and well I didn't expect it to happen at the same time. My partner and I have discussed keeping and not keeping and we will continue to until the date of the scheduled abortion, we've agreed if we skip that appointment then we know what were going to do. I have made adjustments to my lifestyle and have begun a vitamin regimen and am aiming to walk 15k steps a day. But I feel like I'm in this weird pregnancy purgatory.

**[pbu37-0342]** — Longtime martial-arts trainee pays hundreds monthly and initially plans non-contact adaptations. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I spend a couple hundred dollars a month to go to an MMA gym where I do Muay Thai, which I have been training for 8 years. As soon as I got pregnant, my plan was to switch over to bag classes without any physical contact, and modify exercises and intensity as needed.

**[pbu37-0344]** — Loss changes a planned due date and may place the next birth at a new job's start; existing spiral passage excluded. (stage: `trying`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I was expecting baby #2 in February 2027 but recently had a MMC and am now back on the TTC train, unfortunately. 
> 
> If I conceive \*immediately\*, it would be a May baby and this would work out fine, no concerns. However, we all know the unpredictability of pregnancy timing and I fear that more likely the due date of baby will coincide directly with when the new job is supposed to start or at least at a point where I would still like to be on maternity leave. This also means there's a decent chance I will not even be pregnant yet, certainly not to the point of disclosing it to employers, when it's time to sign a new contract.

**[pbu37-0352]** — Persistent nausea forces cancellation of a longstanding paid gym membership despite partial vomiting control. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Today I officially cancelled my gym membership because I just can't use it.
> 
> I'm 16 weeks 4 days today, and since week 7 I have had relentless nausea and vomiting. Vomiting has been controlled with medication (usually throw up once an afternoon instead of multiple times), but I still have horrible nausea all day, and terrible food and water aversions.

## Requests

**190 quote records in the direct product/plan/list/service index.** This deliberately includes requests made to clinicians or relatives, requests for recommendations, practical preparation questions, stated needs for services, care attempts that were refused, and a few explicit contemplated requests. It does not treat every question as demand for a commercial guide. The record’s context and full wording distinguish a direct ask from a wish, an attempted booking, an adopted plan or a retrospective account. A separate broad index after these passages preserves all other overt questions and peer-help wording.

### Direct product, plan, list and service requests, attempts and stated needs

**[pbu20-0102]** — Author repeatedly has to justify consultant-prescribed medication to receptionist. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

I explain I’ve to take my tablets everyday so I can avoid a transfusion and I need them everyday for them to work. She asks who told you that? I said the Obstetrician consultant who wrote in my notes for me to have them and said the gp will be able to refill them for me. Also then proceeded to ask why I think I need them. I again explained I’m almost in my third trimester and need the tablets to help with heart palpitations and energy levels etc for my pregnancy. She keeps asking me who told me to take them and I explain. Eventually she says she’s checking the system then the phone cuts dead. Luckily I was able to make an appointment with a gp for tomorrow through the online triage system. I just hope I can get my medication or unless I’ll be bothering triage for a refill with a 6 hour wait because they didn’t read the notes on the system.

> my OB put me on acyclovir for preventative measures since i have a history of hsv1 (genitally). i RARELY get outbreaks and haven’t had one my entire pregnancy. basically, this medication is making me feel super ill. on top of regular pregnancy symptoms at 38 weeks, i now feel like i have the flu everyday :/ my OB doesn’t have a solution and she’s very risk-adversed. did anyone not take this medication and have a healthy vaginal birth? of course if i felt ANYTHING down there, ill opt for what’s best for my baby.

**[pbu37-0187]** — Employer offers light duty, but obstetric office reportedly declines documentation because nothing is wrong. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I said that my manager/director was absolutely willing to accommodate light duty to keep me in my actual role and that I would need paperwork filled out from my OB. She said that since there’s nothing actually wrong they aren’t able to fill out any paperwork.

**[pbu37-0195]** — Exhaustion and low mood prevent intended pregnancy, career and self-care plans. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I have considered medication, but know I only have a few months until baby is born. Is this normal for 3rd trimester??? I am absolutely useless currently. I had big plans on what I was going to do during pregnancy to support me, baby and career, so far I’ve not managed any of it.

**[pbu37-0198]** — Referral and insurance barriers block dating care in a pregnancy estimated by family at six months. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I have been trying to set up an obgyn appointment but with my insurance it is seemingly impossible. Either places will say they accept my insurance and then tell me they don’t once I call. Or that I need a referral from my pcp. The problem is I haven’t had insurance and years and think I met my pcp only once around 7 years ago. I am trying to get my insurance now to go through Kaiser as I have experience with them and their facilities. My main question is; if I go to an urgent care and explain that I am pregnant and worried due to past miscarriages would I be able to get an ultrasound at an urgent care or ER without needing some referral?

**[pbu37-0207]** — Spontaneous conception after IVF leaves author without clinic support or an established obstetric provider. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> While waiting to get my period to start the mock cycle I realized I was pregnant. My clinic had to drop me because it’s a spontaneous pregnancy. I had a random obgyn run betas and they were fine (88 then 215). Im waiting for a placement ultrasound. 
> 
> Im literally shitting myself. I’m so overwhelmed, I didn’t even pick out an obgyn I was waiting for my clinic to refer me.

**[pbu37-0210]** — Practice closure compounds severe symptoms, prior traumatic birth and difficulty parenting an older daughter. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `experienced_mother`).

> I’m usually good at advocating for myself, but my OB practice is going out of business and that’s added a whole new layer of mental and emotional complications. idk what to do. I had my first daughter with a midwife birth center practice but that ended with a traumatic birth and em C-section and resuscitation with epinephrine. what should I do. 😭 I also feel like I’m a bad mom to my daughter right now.

**[pbu37-0217]** — Author questions preventive aspirin based on age despite two previous uncomplicated pregnancies. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> LONG STORY LONG: Anyone else over 35 prescribed baby aspirin daily JUST because of age with no other risk factors? Obviously will do what is best for baby, but don’t want to take something (especially a NSAID!!!!!) if it could actually hurt baby in any way.

**[pbu37-0219]** — Possible pregnancy creates fear about diet changes, exercise and walking before telling family. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> 2) If I am - I'm freaking out and I don't know what I can and can't do. I don't want to ask my mom, his mom, or tell my friends yet since I'm worried about it being so early and my health really sucks. 
> 
> 3) Should I not change anything with my diet or such? I really wanted to work on my health this summer but now I'm going to be afraid of any change of diet or even exerise will cause me to have a miscarriage.
> 
> 4) I'm suppose to go to Dollywood tomorrow late afternoon with coworkers and I won't really ride any extreme rides but will extreme walking cause harm?

**[pbu37-0227]** — The gestation of a previous missed miscarriage makes the next scan both feared and needed. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m 10w right now and I have an appointment tomorrow, but I stupidly forgot to request an ultrasound with it, so I’m hoping I’ll be able to get an ultrasound as well. The appointment tomorrow is the same appointment timeline in my last pregnancy that I found out the baby stopped developing, and I am an anxious disaster at the moment. I don’t want to have another ultrasound and get the same news I’ve gotten in my last two pregnancies, but I also of course need to know.

**[pbu37-0233]** — Preferred MRI timing conflicts with the reported deadline for considering termination after uncertain brain findings. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> They offered fetal MRI but said they usually prefer to perform it later in pregnancy because the images are more accurate. The problem is that I was also told that if I were to consider termination depending on the MRI findings, I would need to make that decision before 24 weeks. Because of that, I’ve asked if it’s possible to have the MRI scheduled sooner, and I’m currently waiting to hear back.  
> I honestly feel like I’m living in limbo. I love this baby more than anything, but I also want to understand what his future might look like before making any decisions. The uncertainty has been incredibly overwhelming.

**[pbu37-0235]** — Scheduling discussion reduces some infusion fear, but online side-effect accounts and needle anxiety remain. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I did get a chance to speak to someone at the infusion center when they called me yesterday to go over scheduling and I was able to ask some questions and definitely feel less nervous than I was, buttt I’m still definitely pretty nervous. My first appointment won’t be until the very end of this month, since apparently they’re completely booked out with patients this month. I’ve read online that some people have some pretty crazy side effects from the infusions. Has anyone here experienced that? I’m just anxious since this is the first time I’ve had to get any type of infusion and I’m also terrified of needles so that doesn’t help. I’d like to at least try to prepare myself a bit for what could happen!

**[pbu37-0236]** — Author explicitly asks whether optional weekly reassurance scans relieve or increase anxiety. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m wondering whether I should book a 6-week scan, or if it’s better to just wait until 7 weeks. I hate feeling like I’m just sitting in limbo. Did anyone else in a similar situation have weekly reassurance scans, and did it help your anxiety or just make you more anxious?

**[pbu37-0248]** — Author seeks a cervical check after activity-related pressure despite reassurance and a known syndrome. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> It’s really making me anxious more than I was before this started, and safe to say I was extremely worried before this too. My midwife and my OB says it’s normal for this gestation but I can’t help thinking if it really is… 
> 
> I have a syndrome that can cause issues with shortened cervix and preterm labour/late miscarriage. I’m going to the OB in two days to get my cervix checked (after a lot of convincing from me). I’m worried that it will be ”too late” if something bad is going on?

**[pbu37-0254]** — Author requests concrete post-D&C timelines, investigations and healthy-pregnancy stories. (stage: `trying`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I was hoping to hear from others:  
> 1) When did you ovulate after your D&C?  
> 2) How long did it take you to conceive again?  
> 3) Did anyone conceive before their first period?  
> 4) If you had a miscarriage in your first pregnancy, did you go on to have a healthy pregnancy afterward?  
> 5) Were there any tests or changes your doctor recommended after a first miscarriage?

**[pbu37-0262]** — Spotting prompts an immediate flight purchase and uncertainty about emergency care versus calling the next morning. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m currently on a flight home, im so scared that I literally bought a plane ticket not even an hour after I saw the blood. I’m wondering if I should go to the emergency as soon as I land or wait till tomorrow Monday morning and call my obgyn. I don’t think I’ve had much bleeding right now, I haven’t felt anything, but I’m also very scared to go check. :(
> 
> What do yall think would be best, ER tonight or calling my OB tomorrow morning?

**[pbu37-0265]** — Author questions exercise restriction intended partly to reduce distress because stopping exercise would itself increase distress. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Doctor recommended exercise restrictions (no heavy lifting/vigorous cardio) until bleeding resolves, but then made it sound—though was a little squishy about this!—like heavy lifting would have no effect on miscarriage risk and was just to reduce the mental stress to me of the possibility of more bleeding. 
> 
> I don’t really care about the mental stress of bleeding—for me, the mental stress of exercise restriction will be just as high if not higher—but I of course do care about miscarriage risk. 
> 
> Anyone else been in a similar situation? What did your doctor recommend? What do you make of the seemingly very limited data about SCH and exercise restriction?

**[pbu37-0266]** — Mother reports intrusive breastfeeding handling and blame for not pumping when no pump was available. (stage: `newborn_0_2_weeks`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Trying to learn to breast feed him In the nursery pulling the curtain closed in pain constantly three or four people walking in telling me what to do, at one point the one nurse walked in grabbed my tit and shoved it in my sons mouth, who I’m still trying to figure out how to hold in the first place, he’s tiny  
> Ask for a pump they don’t have any left just for a nurse to be like you should have been pumping this whole time

**[pbu37-0272]** — Time and cost of repeated blood tests make early symptom assessment difficult. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I called my obgyn and since I’m not yet at the 8 week mark, if they were to get me in I would have to go in every other day to get blood drawn for tests, and I don’t have the time nor the money for that. I could also go to the ER if the pain were to get worse, but at this stage, they wouldn’t even be able to see much. I don’t have women around me that I can ask for advice, so I’m hoping someone here is willing to share their experience and maybe help me realize whether this is normal or I should seek immediate help.

**[pbu37-0273]** — Previous traumatic birth leads partner to resist prenatal care while author seeks monitoring. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I brought up going to a midwife in another town less than an hour away and my husband flipped out a bit. Saying he thinks I should just have faith in this pregnancy and that if I get an OB it means I'm handing over my pregnancy and my body to them to do and decide what they want. He's arguably feeling very protective of me and the baby after the trauma we all endured the first time but his reaction to me feeling like I need prenatal care was a bit jarring and unfair as it feels really important to make sure the pregnancy is progressing well especially AFTER 20w when high blood pressure could be early onset pre-e or a sign of it. 
> 
> Ultimately after some discussion he agreed with me that if it helps with anxiety seeing a midwife shouldn't do any harm, but it was incredibly emotional having to explain to him that it feels like my body failed me and my son by not being able to grow him to full term size in the safety of my belly and putting us through all that hospital trauma in the first place.

**[pbu37-0274]** — Author seeks a documented negative THC test to counter fear about an earlier positive result; policy understanding is unverified. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I’m thinking I want to ask my midwife if we can do another urine drug test so that I can have that negative on my chart. I just want it to be on record that I stopped using THC upon finding out I was pregnant and this test will show that. I worry about CPS involvement because of that one positive early on, but I know that in Ohio CPS only gets involved if me or the baby test positive for anything at birth which won’t be a problem in my case. My question is, am I doing too much? Is that weird to request a drug test? lol or is the negative drug test a good idea?

**[pbu37-0279]** — During prolonged screening uncertainty, the only available family support reopens old distress. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> In a desperate need for help I called my mom to come stay with me. She isn’t the best company as she has given me so much trauma but I literally had no one else. But I am very anxious still. And stressed. My mom triggers my old wounds and expects me to regulate her emotions while I am itself a mess. I can’t ask her to leave because she flew from another country to be with me.

**[pbu37-0280]** — Author considers restarting an SSRI to sleep, eat and function during pregnancy. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I was on Sertraline pre pregnancy and stopped after my pregnancy. But I am thinking of taking some now because I really need to sleep and stop crying. I am unable to function and eat for my baby. I feel like my mind is failing me and I feel like a failure for not being able to take care of myself. Did anyone start SSRI’s in the middle of their pregnancy?? Has it affected your baby ?

**[pbu37-0294]** — Author reports doctors recommending follow-up with partner and discussion of anxiety medication; this is testimony, not verified advice. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Edit: current recommendation from my doctors is take it DAY BY DAY. they recommend I wait for my partner to return. because I am currently uncertain of even continuing the pregnancy, they have left it to my choice. they informed me of the risks of smoking while pregnant, and recommend I come back in with my partner to discuss medications to reduce anxiety.

**[pbu37-0295]** — Pregnant friend asks how to support detox and disclosure without amplifying shame. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> In the meantime, I’m trying to figure out how I can support her and validate her fears about how all of this has already affected her baby without contributing to a spiral of shame and despair. All of her scans have gone well and same with every OB appointment. I told her she should report her drug and alcohol use to her OB asap so they can take the necessary steps to make sure her baby is ok.
> 
> I know next to nothing about cps laws/procedures, or really too much about addiction so I’m at a loss on how to support my friend

**[pbu37-0304]** — Family funeral timing complicates a membrane sweep and hoped-for spontaneous labour before a planned caesarean. (stage: `third_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> The last piece of this is that my **MILs service is scheduled for this upcoming Thursday, 9/17/26. My appointment and optional membrane sweep is tomorrow, 9/14/26.** From what I have read, if the Bishop score is favorable, there is approximately a 50/50 chance of going into labor within 48 hours of the membrane sweep. 
> 
> My husband and I are really hoping for labor, but I want to be able to be at the service to support our family. However, my next appointment would be 9/21 with a possible c-section date of 9/24, and I would rather not cut it that close if there’s a possibility of labor. 
> 
> What would you do? Any suggestions are appreciated.

**[pbu37-0308]** — Author with EDS and suspected MCAS cannot find comparable experiences of widespread nighttime pain. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Any suggestions? I’m using magnesium spray, I’ve been taking doxepin which is an antidepressant and antihistamine nightly for about a year now, idk what else to try. I do have EDS and suspected MCAS. I can’t find anyone who went through this online anywhere. Thanks so much.

**[pbu37-0309]** — Seventeen-year-old asks for packing help and kindness after criticism creates shame. (stage: `second_trimester`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Hi, I'm new here and this is my first. I'm 17, and 20 weeks pregnant, I live with my aunt and my fiancé-who is the child's father. ￼I'm starting to work on a hospital bag, but I have no idea what to pack so I thought I'd join a group and get some suggestions. Please be nice, I'm still navigating this and I'm very emotional and scared. I've had enough people be mean to me that I'm starting to be ashamed and I don't want that.

**[pbu37-0310]** — Fear of pregnancy, costs and a religious mother's reaction delays taking a test. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Im the (18 y/o) daughter of a very religious mother. Love her to death but I think she would kill me if I was pregnant. The reason I think I may be is because im quite late to my period, but theres also a chance thats because I recently stopped taking my birth control because it was causing issues. I havent taken a test because im far to anxious so im kinda just waiting it out. Im not financially stable enough to even give birth let alone keep the child so id prefer to get an abortion if I am in fact pregnant, but as previously stated, my mom is super religious… please help me figure out what to do because im so scared.q

**[pbu37-0316]** — Insurance denial then a sample/fax mismatch delay genetic testing even after author agrees to self-pay. (stage: `second_pregnancy`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Fast forward two weeks, insurance denied it because it isn’t medically necessary. At that point I said fine, I will pay out of pocket if it means we can start the testing. GC said testing would start that Monday. 8 days later, I’ve heard nothing so I reach out. Unfortunately they didn’t connect the fax to start testing to my sample so it never was started but they assured me it would be ready in 3-5 days. We’re now past that window, and tomorrow makes 4 weeks of waiting.

**[pbu37-0317]** — Unanswered referral and insurance resources coincide with little baby-shower response and a sense of having no village. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> I was supposed to get a referral to behavioral health but no one called, none of the resources my insurance offered responded, I have no village, all my family lives too far away and so do my friends, the friends I have close by don’t care much anymore and only 4 people responded to my baby shower invite so I’m likely gonna cancel it.  I dont truly know why I created a registry when no one is going to really be supporting us anyways.

**[pbu37-0320]** — A maternity-benefit waiting period makes pregnancy timing a choice about interim costs and unplanned emergencies. (stage: `trying`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> My insurance confirmed with me in writing that even if I conceive prior to April the 2nd, my benefits will still become active on that date. So I know that insurance will cover the delivery, but I’ll have to pay out of pocket if I become pregnant prior to April 2nd for OB visits/care. I know I can probably manage to pay for a few month out of pocket for care but what if something emergent happens because of pregnancy? Like a hospital visit or a miscarriage. Or if theres something wrong with the baby I’ll need more testing for. I want to trust my body that everything will be okay and I can get pregnant and just wait it out until April the 2nd but I also feel irresponsible and part of me thinks I should wait. I really want a baby now and want to start trying. Thoughts?

**[pbu37-0326]** — Author asks about absent periods nine weeks after D&C while awaiting a doctor's response. (stage: `looking_back`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> Has anyone not gotten a period 9wks after a d&c for an early pregnancy loss? I still haven’t gotten mine and I’m starting to stress. I’ve reached out to my doctor but just curious what others have experienced

**[pbu37-0331]** — Primary care refuses Adderall while obstetrician advises continuing it for depression, leaving author unsure after previous losses. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> My pcp is telling me absolutely not and they are refusing to prescribe, my obgyn is telling me to continue taking it as it is actually used off label to treat my depression symptoms (major depressive disorder). I have had three miscarriages in the past and don’t want to add unnecessary risk to the babies development as we have been trying for over a year this time to get pregnant.

**[pbu37-0340]** — Major job offer, physical travel, benefit uncertainty and disclosure timing complicate whether to continue pregnancy. (stage: `first_trimester`; speaker: `expecting_mother`).

> I work in outside sales and that requires me to visit offices with samples and present them. It also requires extra social functions such as dinners, happy hours, etc. What really throws a wrench into things is that I am in the midst of finalizing a job offer which would take my base salary from 125k to 180k... life changing, nevermind the commission. I have already received a verbal offer and we discussed a start date of 1/2. The job is technically remote but it requires visits to clients multiple times a week within the NYC area. Sometimes pulling heavy samples across the city, othertimes leading social functions after hours. I would also have to do 2 weeks of training at the main office in another state at the start. I am terrified of losing the opportunity or not performing as expected due to the pregnancy. I wish I was a few months into the job.. but I guess we don't always get to choose when these things happen. I haven't received the benefits package yet at the new company but I am extermely focused on understanding the lapse in insurance, and the best way to cover it (cobra, market place ins, etc). I am also aware that to qualify for FMLA I need to work for an employer for a year which also worries me as I have not reviewed the new maternity policy yet for the new company. Staying at my current role is not an option because the job opportunity is too financially beneficial. Beyond that I am struggling with when is the right time to notify the new company. I would be 16/17 weeks during the training and likley showing or just starting to show. I should be receiving the written offer the week before the scheduled abortion. I am currently 190 lbs and have considered if I can maintain a healthy first trimester and wear oversized clothes, I can maybe count on hiding it and notifiying them later. I don't want to be deceitful and I know legalllyyyyy they can't rescind the offer based on pregancy. The woman I'd be reporting to has kids and that is a plus but I am so scared I'm going to ruin things or upset them.

**[pbu37-0346]** — Author asks the father to co-parent rather than resume the relationship. (stage: `unknown`; speaker: `parent_unspecified`).

> i tried to put my pride aside yesterday and se for one last time could we co parent because im not asking to be with him but because he has a son out here that need both parents …
